However, they typically move directly from a PDE problem to solver code, leaving the solver strategy implicit in implementation details. Feedback from a failed solve is therefore routed back to code edits rather than to the underlying strategy, so numerical decisions remain hard to check before code is generated and hard to revise using numerical evidence when it fails. To address this limitation, we propose AutoPDE, a code agent that maintains the solver strategy as an explicitly represented object throughout the solving process: an independent, inspectable object that is built before any code is written and can be revised, using numerical evidence, whenever a solve fails. AutoPDE builds and maintains this object in three stages, all drawing from a library of reusable PDE-solving skills: PDE analysis identifies the equation type and algebraic structure; numerical method selection chooses a numerical method that matches the analysis result and commits to a discretization, stabilization, and linear solver accordingly; and adaptive tuning runs low-cost pilot solves to calibrate resolution and tolerances under the prescribed accuracy and runtime budget. We evaluate AutoPDE on the PDE Agent Bench, where experimental results show that AutoPDE achieves a pass rate of $54.5%$, improving over the strongest baseline by $14.2$ percentage points.

</details>


### [101] [Accelerating NeurASP with vectorization and caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10787)
*Alexander Philipp Rader,Alessandra Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过向量化、批处理和中间计算缓存等优化手段，显著提升了神经符号AI框架NeurASP的计算性能，在大规模任务上实现了多个数量级的加速，并提出了一个基于扑克牌的新难度数据集来验证改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeurASP框架在通过不可微的ASP组件进行反向传播时，需要进行昂贵的概率和梯度计算，这严重限制了其向更复杂任务的可扩展性。现有实现在面对较大规模任务时计算效率低下，成为实际应用的瓶颈。

Method: 针对NeurASP训练过程中的计算瓶颈，提出三项关键优化策略：（1）向量化（vectorization）——将概率和梯度计算转化为向量运算以提高并行效率；（2）批处理（batch processing）——支持批量样本的同时处理；（3）中间计算缓存（caching of intermediate computations）——在训练过程中缓存重复计算的中间结果以避免冗余计算。同时提出了一个基于扑克牌的新难度任务数据集用于测试。

Result: 与原始NeurASP实现相比，优化后的新实现在较大规模任务上实现了多个数量级的计算加速。通过新提出的扑克牌难度数据集，验证了增强后学习函数的能力。

Conclusion: 通过工程优化手段（向量化、批处理、缓存），可以有效解决NeurASP的可扩展性瓶颈，使其能够处理更复杂的神经符号任务。新提出的扑克牌数据集为评估NeurASP在困难任务上的表现提供了有效的测试基准。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic AI combines neural networks with symbolic programs to create robust and explainable predictions. One such framework is NeurASP, which trains a neural network to predict concepts and reasons over them using rules written in answer set programming (ASP) to solve downstream tasks. Crucially, labels are only provided for the downstream prediction produced by the symbolic rules, not for the latent concepts themselves.Backpropagation through the non-differentiable ASP component requires expensive probability and gradient calculations, which has hindered scalability to more sophisticated tasks.In this paper, we address the current limitations of NeurASP by improving its computational performance through vectorization, batch processing and caching of intermediate computations during training. We compare computation speeds between the original and our new implementation of NeurASP and report speedups of multiple orders of magnitude for larger tasks. To this end, we propose a new dataset of difficult tasks involving playing cards, which we use to test the capabilities of NeurASP's enhanced learning function.

</details>


### [102] [READER: Robust Evidence-based Authorship Decoding via Extracted Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10794)
*Jiaxu Liu,Sunnan Mu,Dong Huang,Liuyin Wang,Jing Shao,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出READER框架，通过冻结的代理LLM的内部表示空间，结合贝叶斯证据累积机制，实现对黑盒LLM输出的动态来源识别，在50个目标模型的Agent500数据集上取得显著优于句子编码器基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体应用日益通过官方和第三方LLM API路由用户任务，模型来源追溯成为关键的运维问题：给定一个黑盒响应，如何判断它由哪个模型生成？现有方法依赖固定输入集或基准测试套件，但在真实场景中，提示是动态变化、非预定义的。这种设定下，提示语义主导生成文本，而模型特有的作者痕迹在表面层面微弱且不一致，使得来源识别极具挑战性。

Method: 提出READER（基于鲁棒证据的作者身份解码框架），核心包含三个组件：(1) 将黑盒输出映射到冻结代理LLM的激活空间；(2) 对每个响应内的token状态进行时序过滤；(3) 采用贝叶斯证据累积策略，对独立采样的多个提示的单次响应对数后验证据进行求和，避免了对提示特定表示进行脆弱的均值池化，同时保留了逐查询的证据以实现校准的置信度估计。

Result: 在Agent500（包含50个目标模型、基于智能体风格提示构建的数据集）上，READER仅凭单次响应即可达到31.0%-42.4%的top-1准确率，使用50次响应时提升至70.0%-84.0%，大幅超越句子编码器指纹方法。跨9个代理读取器的扩展实验进一步表明，更强的LLM能暴露出更多线性可解码的作者身份结构。

Conclusion: 作者身份感知能力已经内在存在于冻结LLM的表示中，无需专门训练即可被提取利用。通过贝叶斯证据累积的多查询归因策略，READER能够将微弱的单次模型指纹信号有效聚合为可靠的来源判定，为动态黑盒LLM来源追溯提供了实用的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: As agentic applications increasingly route user tasks through official and third-party LLM APIs, provenance becomes an operational question: which model generated a given black-box response? We study Dynamic Black-Box LLM Provenance: identifying the source LLM from generations elicited by query-varying, non-predefined prompts rather than a fixed input set or benchmark suite. This setting is difficult because prompt semantics dominate the text, while model-specific authorship traces are weak and inconsistent at the surface level. We introduce READER (Robust Evidence-based Authorship Decoding via Extracted Representations), a lightweight provenance framework that treats a frozen proxy LLM as a reader of hidden authorship evidence. READER maps black-box outputs into proxy activation space, temporally filters token states within each response, and performs Bayesian Evidence Accumulation by summing single-response log-posterior evidence across independently sampled prompts. This avoids fragile mean-pooling of prompt-specific representations while preserving the query-wise evidence needed for calibrated confidence. On Agent500, a 50-target dataset built from agent-style prompts, READER reaches $31.0$-$42.4\%$ top-1 accuracy from a single response and $70.0$-$84.0\%$ from 50 responses, substantially outperforming sentence-encoder fingerprints. Scaling across nine proxy readers further shows that stronger LLMs expose more linearly decodable authorship structure, suggesting that authorship perception is already present in frozen LLM representations and can be converted into reliable multi-query attribution.

</details>


### [103] [Evaluating Research-Level Math Proofs via Strict Step-Level Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10799)
*Yifeng Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种严格的逐步验证框架，替代全局评估方法，解决大语言模型在复杂数学证明验证中的"上下文污染"问题，通过约束每步推理的定理来源，显著提升对微妙逻辑错误的定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在验证复杂数学证明时面临"上下文污染"问题：全局评估方式下，表面合理的陈述会掩盖微妙的逻辑缺陷，导致模型产生幻觉或过度怀疑。现有方法无法可靠地定位研究级证明中的细微逻辑错误，亟需一种更精细、更严格的验证范式。

Method: 从全局评估转向严格的步级验证（step-level verification）：为每个推导步骤维护详细的上下文信息，并严格约束每步所应用定理的来源。在基于FirstProof挑战构建的精心策划的对抗性诊断测试集（研究级证明）上进行评估，并通过系统性消融实验验证各推理约束的必要性。引导智能体以类似人类数学家的谨慎方式组织其验证笔记。

Result: 系统消融实验表明，推理约束不可或缺——无约束的全局提示方法始终无法定位微妙的逻辑错误。该方法不仅优于全局评估，还从根本上改变了失败的分类体系：剩余的误拒主要表现为"学究式过度严格"（pedantic hyper-rigor），源于未明确声明的领域惯例，实质上暴露了专家基准本身的隐含歧义，而非严重的逻辑幻觉。

Conclusion: 引导智能体以谨慎、类人数学家的方式组织验证过程，能够显著提升其区分严格证明与错误证明的能力。该方法有望增强智能体在基础模型尚未充分掌握的前沿数学概念上的推理能力，并为未来自动化证明审查系统奠定理论基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to rigorously verify complex mathematical proofs. Standard global evaluation approaches suffer from "context poisoning," in which superficially plausible statements mask subtle logical flaws, leading to hallucination or over-skepticism. To address this, we shift from global evaluation to strict step-level verification: our framework maintains detailed context for each deduction step and strictly constrains the sources of applied theorems. We evaluate on a carefully curated adversarial diagnostic suite of research-level proofs drawn from the FirstProof challenge. A systematic ablation study demonstrates that these deductive constraints are indispensable, as unconstrained global prompting consistently fails to localize subtle logical errors. Beyond outperforming global evaluation, our approach fundamentally alters the failure taxonomy. Error analysis reveals that, rather than exhibiting severe logical hallucinations, remaining rejections are primarily instances of "pedantic hyper-rigor" stemming from unstated domain conventions, effectively exposing implicit ambiguities within the expert benchmark itself. Our findings suggest that prompting agents to organize their verification notes in a cautious, human-mathematician-like manner can substantially improve their ability to distinguish rigorous proofs from flawed ones, with the potential to strengthen agentic reasoning on frontier mathematical concepts that the base model does not already know well, and to lay a theoretical foundation for future automated proof-review systems. Code and prompts are available at GitHub.

</details>


### [104] [Moonshine: An Autonomous Mathematical Research Agent Centered on Conjecture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10806)
*Xiaoyang Chen,Xiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了自主数学猜想生成智能体Moonshine，它将经典Jacobian猜想的核心逻辑迁移到单隐层仿射脊sigmoid神经网络上，提出了"神经Jacobian猜想"（NJC），并借助GPT-5.5-pro和DeepSeek-V4-pro分别独立完成了N=n+1情形的完整证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI数学系统多以求解单一命题为终点，缺乏自主生成有数学意义的猜想、构建可扩展理论框架的能力。本文旨在展示Moonshine智能体如何从经典问题（Jacobian猜想）中提取结构，蒸馏新概念，并自主提出具有数学意义的新猜想，从而推动AI驱动的数学发现范式。

Method: Moonshine通过三步工作流程运作：猜想生成、桥梁构建与障碍识别。具体地，它将Jacobian猜想的核心逻辑——"局部非退化能否强制全局单射性"——迁移到单隐层仿射脊sigmoid网络上，提出神经Jacobian猜想（NJC）：若此类网络在全空间上具有严格正的Jacobian行列式，则其必为全局单射。随后分别调用GPT-5.5-pro和DeepSeek-V4-pro独立给出N=n+1情形的完整证明，并通过与GPT-5.5-pro的交互式web界面协作，开发了一个几何-拓扑证明。

Result: 对于宽度N=n+1的情形，Moonshine通过两个独立的大语言模型（GPT-5.5-pro和DeepSeek-V4-pro）分别获得了NJC的完整证明，并额外通过交互式协作获得了一个几何-拓扑证明路径。这些结果为该猜想的合理性提供了初步证据。一般高宽度情形（N≥n+2）仍未解决。

Conclusion: 本工作展示了Moonshine智能体自主生成有意义数学问题并在其上取得严格进展的能力。NJC作为Jacobian猜想在神经网络领域的类比，为理解神经网络的全局单射性提供了新视角。N=n+1情形的成功证明验证了猜想的合理性，而N≥n+2的一般情形留待后续研究。

Abstract: Moonshine is an autonomous agent whose central objective is to generate mathematical conjectures. Its core capability is to extract structure from classical problems, distill new concepts, and formulate conjectures of mathematical significance. Rather than treating the solution of a single proposition as its endpoint, Moonshine builds an extensible theoretical framework through conjecture generation, bridge building, and obstacle identification. This article uses Moonshine's exploration of the Jacobian conjecture as an example. It shows how the central logic of whether local nondegeneracy can force global injectivity is transferred to one-hidden-layer affine-ridge sigmoid networks. This leads to the formulation of the \emph{Neural Jacobian Conjecture} (NJC): if such a network has strictly positive Jacobian determinant on the whole space, then it must be globally injective. By invoking GPT-5.5-pro and DeepSeek-V4-pro separately, Moonshine obtained independent complete proofs for the case \(N=n+1\). In addition, with the assistance of ChatGPT through interactive use of its web interface with GPT-5.5-pro, a geometric-topological proof was developed. These results provide preliminary evidence for the plausibility of the conjecture. The general higher-width case \(N\ge n+2\), however, remains unresolved and is left for further investigation. This work illustrates Moonshine's ability to autonomously generate meaningful mathematical problems and make rigorous progress on them.

</details>


### [105] [Do VLMs Reason Like Engineers? A Benchmark and a Stage-wise Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10833)
*Syed Wasiq,Syed Mohamad Tawseeq,Yashwant Pravinrao Bangde,Debaditya Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EngVQA基准测试和8阶段自动评估框架，用于细粒度评估视觉语言模型在工程推理任务中的中间过程表现，揭示了当前模型在工程推理能力上的显著局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型(VLM)在通用多模态推理基准上表现良好，但其工程推理能力尚未被充分探索。工程问题求解需要解读技术图表、选择物理原理、保持物理一致性的多步推理，这些能力对工程教育、科学辅助和技术决策至关重要。现有基准主要评估最终答案，对中间推理过程的评估不足，推理失败可能产生物理上无效但表面上合理的解答。

Method: 提出EngVQA多模态基准，涵盖5个工程学科共696道问题；设计8阶段自动评估框架，独立评估解题过程中每个阶段（而非仅评估最终答案），实现对推理失败的细粒度分析。对多个开源和闭源SOTA VLM进行基准测试。

Result: 当前SOTA视觉语言模型在工程推理能力上存在显著局限性。人工评估与自动评估框架高度一致，Pearson相关系数达0.975，在10分制评分中平均绝对误差仅为0.67。

Conclusion: 过程导向的评估方法对于可靠评估多模态工程推理系统至关重要。当前VLM在工程推理方面仍存在实质性不足，EngVQA基准和提出的评估框架为该领域提供了有效的细粒度诊断工具。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate strong performance on general multimodal reasoning benchmarks, yet their ability to perform engineering reasoning remains largely unexplored. Unlike general visual question answering, engineering problem solving requires interpreting technical diagrams, selecting governing physical principles, and maintaining physically consistent multi-step reasoning. These capabilities are increasingly important for AI systems used in engineering education, scientific assistance, and technical decision-making, where reasoning failures may produce physically invalid yet superficially plausible solutions. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate final answers and provide limited assessment of intermediate reasoning processes. We introduce EngVQA, a multimodal benchmark for evaluating engineering reasoning across 5 engineering subjects containing 696 problems. We introduce an 8-stage automatic evaluation framework for assessing VLM-generated solutions. The framework independently evaluates each stage of the solution, enabling fine-grained analysis of reasoning failures. We benchmark multiple state-of-the-art open and closed source VLMs on our evaluation framework and demonstrate substantial limitations in current engineering reasoning capabilities. Human evaluation shows strong agreement with our automated framework, achieving a Pearson correlation of 0.975 and a mean absolute error of 0.67 on a 10-point grading scale. Our results highlight the importance of process-oriented evaluation for reliable assessment of multimodal engineering reasoning systems.

</details>


### [106] [Large-scale semantic mapping of learner agency and autonomy reveals what measurement and generative AI research overlook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10881)
*Fei Qin,Xiaobo Liu,Yaowen Zhang,Xuming Li,Fei Wang,Mutlu Cukurova,Jingjing Chen,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过语义分析流水线对14000余篇文献中的学习者能动性（agency）和自主性（autonomy）定义及量表条目进行大规模实证分析，揭示了两个构念的"叮当谬误"（jingle-jangle fallacy），并将其定义景观归纳为三个维度：任务层面的学习调控、个体层面的内在动机与决策、社会文化层面的关系性行动。研究发现现有量表系统性地低估了社会文化维度，而当前生成式AI教育研究则过度集中于学习调控维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习者能动性（learner agency）和自主性（learner autonomy）是个体发展的基础概念，但学界普遍存在"叮当谬误"——相同术语指代不同构念、不同术语指代相同构念——严重阻碍了知识的累积性建构。研究者需要系统性地厘清这两个构念在实际学术使用中的语义边界与重叠，以推动概念化、测量和实践的发展。

Method: 研究采用"意义由语言实践中的使用所构成"的立场，从超过14000篇出版物中提取了8954条定义和2700个量表条目，构建了一套语义分析流水线（semantic analysis pipeline），对研究者实际使用学习者能动性和自主性的方式进行大规模、数据驱动的实证考察。

Result: 两个构念的定义景观可归结为三个维度：（1）学习的调控与控制（任务维度）；（2）内在动机与内部决策（个体维度）；（3）社会关系性行动（社会文化维度）。该结果实证量化了叮当谬误的存在。然而，现有测量量表系统性地低估了社会文化维度。此外，当前教育领域的生成式AI研究主要集中在学习调控维度，窄化了AI中介学习环境旨在培养的行为范畴。

Conclusion: 本研究超越了单纯的概念澄清，对学习者能动性和自主性的概念化、测量工具开发以及教育实践具有直接启示意义：应重视其多维度特性，尤其需补足社会文化维度在量表设计和AI教育应用中的缺失，以支持对学习者多维能动性和自主性的全面培养。

Abstract: Learner agency and autonomy are foundational to personal development, yet a pervasive "jingle-jangle" fallacy (i.e. identical terms denoting different constructs, distinct terms denoting identical ones) has substantially hindered cumulative knowledge. Treating meaning as a phenomenon constituted through use in linguistic practice, we extracted 8,954 definitions and 2,700 scale items from over 14,000 publications, to investigate how researchers actually used learner agency and autonomy with a semantic analysis pipeline. The definitional landscape of two constructs resolves into three dimensions: regulation and control of learning (task), intrinsic motivation and internal decision-making (person), and social-relational action (sociocultural), thereby empirically quantifying the jingle-jangle fallacy. Existing scales, however, systematically underrepresent the sociocultural dimension. Critically, current generative AI research in education concentrates on learning regulation and control, narrowing the behavioral repertoire that AI-mediated learning environments are designed to cultivate. Beyond conceptual clarification, this work carries direct implications for conceptualization, measurement, and practice towards supporting the multidimensional learner agency and autonomy.

</details>


### [107] [Role-Agent: Bootstrapping LLM Agents via Dual-Role Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10917)
*Xucong Wang,Ziyu Ma,Shidong Yang,Tongwen Huang,Pengkun Wang,Yong Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Role-Agent提出一种让单个LLM同时扮演智能体和环境的框架，通过世界模型预测提供过程奖励和失败模式分析重塑训练分布，实现智能体与环境的协同进化，在多个基准上平均提升超过4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的学习受限于低效的交互反馈和静态训练环境，难以实现更广泛的泛化能力。具体而言，交互反馈不够充分无法有效指导学习，训练环境固定不变无法针对性地提升智能体的薄弱环节。

Method: 提出Role-Agent框架，利用单个LLM同时充当智能体和环境，包含两个协同组件：(1) World-In-Agent (WIA)：LLM作为智能体在每步动作后预测未来状态，将预测状态与实际状态的对齐程度作为过程奖励，鼓励环境感知的推理能力；(2) Agent-In-World (AIW)：LLM分析失败轨迹中的失败模式，并检索具有相似失败模式的任务，重塑训练数据分布以进行针对性练习。两个组件形成自举式协同进化机制。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，Role-Agent相比强基线方法持续提升性能，平均增益超过4%。

Conclusion: 通过让单个LLM同时承担智能体和环境的双重角色，Role-Agent有效解决了交互反馈低效和训练环境静态的问题，实现了智能体的自举式协同进化，证明了环境感知推理奖励和基于失败模式的训练分布重塑对提升LLM智能体性能的有效性。

Abstract: Although Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong performance on complex tasks, their learning is often limited by inefficient interaction feedback and static training environments, which hinder broader generalization. To address these limitations, this paper introduces Role-Agent, \textcolor{black}{a framework} that harnesses a single LLM to function concurrently as both the agent and the environment, enabling a bootstrapped co-evolution. Role-Agent comprises two synergistic components: World-In-Agent (WIA) and Agent-In-World (AIW). In WIA, the LLM acts as the agent and predicts future states after each action; the alignment between predicted and actual states is then used as a process reward, encouraging environment-aware reasoning. In AIW, the LLM analyzes failure modes from failed trajectories and retrieves tasks with similar failure patterns, thereby reshaping the training data distribution for targeted practice. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that Role-Agent consistently improves performance, yielding an average gain of over 4\% over strong baselines.

</details>


### [108] [Frontier Coding Agents Use Metaprogramming to Adapt to Unfamiliar Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10933)
*Aman Sharma,Sushrut Thorat,Paras Chopra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过四种深奥编程语言评估六种编码智能体，发现强智能体会自发采用元编程策略（用Python生成目标语言代码），这种能力差异在主流基准测试中被压缩，且额外计算资源只能放大已有策略而非创造新策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码智能体通常在熟悉的软件环境（主流语言、常见库、公开仓库）中评估，这些基准测试会掩盖智能体面对不熟悉语言时的真实行为差异。作者希望通过使用深奥编程语言（esoteric programming languages）来暴露不同智能体之间被主流基准（如SWE-Bench Verified和Terminal-Bench 2.0）压缩到狭窄区间的能力差距。

Method: 使用四种深奥编程语言（包括Brainfuck和Befunge-98等），采用顺序设置（含文件编辑、本地执行和隐藏测试评分）评估六种当代编码智能体。实验设计包括：(1) 观察智能体自发采用的策略；(2) 禁止元编程策略后测量性能下降；(3) 将强智能体的文本指导蒸馏给弱智能体；(4) 提供Opus衍生的Python辅助代码（不含已解决的基准程序或隐藏测试答案）给较弱智能体；(5) 变化解释器调用次数和输出token数量观察效果。

Result: （1）最强智能体（Claude Opus 4.6和GPT-5.4 xhigh）经常避免直接编写目标语言，转而编写生成目标语言代码的Python程序并本地调试；（2）禁止元编程策略导致大幅性能下降；（3）从该策略蒸馏的文本指导对弱智能体无实质改善；（4）Opus衍生的Python辅助代码显著提升Sonnet 4.6和GPT-5.4 mini的表现，但Haiku 4.5仍然很低；（5）更多解释器调用和输出token能改善强智能体但对弱智能体几乎无效，表明资源放大有用策略而非创造策略。

Conclusion: 强编码智能体通过利用工具、反馈和工作空间状态来构建目标语言的工作模型，从而适应不熟悉的语言。元编程是最明显的案例，但更根本的能力差距在于构建和调试一个在目标语言规则下有效的策略。深奥语言基准能有效区分智能体的适应性能力，揭示出主流基准测试无法捕捉的差异。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents are usually evaluated in familiar software settings: mainstream languages, common libraries, and public repositories. These benchmarks remain important, but they can hide how agents behave when the language itself is unfamiliar. We evaluate six contemporary coding agents on four esoteric programming languages using a sequential setup with file editing, local execution, and hidden-test grading. Our protocol exposes capability differences between these agents that mainstream coding and agentic benchmarks such as SWE-Bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.0 compress into much narrower bands. We observe that the strongest agents, Claude Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.4 xhigh, often avoid writing the target language directly. On Brainfuck and Befunge-98, they write Python programs that generate target-language code and debug those generators locally. Forbidding this metaprogramming strategy causes large performance drops. Text guidance distilled from this strategy does not materially improve weaker agents. In contrast, Opus-derived Python helper code for building generators, with no solved benchmark programs or hidden-test answers, sharply improves Sonnet 4.6 and GPT-5.4 mini on the same problems, while Haiku 4.5 remains low. More interpreter calls and output tokens improve stronger agents but leave weaker agents near their original performance, indicating that these resources amplify useful strategies rather than create them. Together, these results show that strong coding agents adapt to unfamiliar languages by using tools, feedback, and workspace state to build a working model of the target language. Metaprogramming is the clearest case, but the broader gap is constructing and debugging a strategy that works under the target language's rules.

</details>


### [109] [WorldKernel: A World Model is the Coupling Kernel of Admissible Possible Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10934)
*Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了预测模型在反事实推理中的根本失败模式——无法表征跨反事实世界耦合的不确定性，并提出以半正定耦合核K(T,T')为核心的世界模型理论框架，利用半正定性约束、本体论公理和定向疤痕学习来界定反事实量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普遍假设认为足够的观测和干预数据配合强预测器即可解决因果推断问题。但本文发现一个结构性失败模式：对于不可识别量（反事实世界间的耦合），即使是强预测器和贝叶斯基线也会崩溃为点估计（28%的模型中甚至给出任何有效模型都无法产生的值），而真实答案是一个更多数据永远无法缩小的容许区间。这一差距是结构性的——预测本身无法表征跨反事实世界耦合上的不确定性。

Method: 将世界模型形式化为在容许世界上的单一半正定耦合核K(T,T')：其对角线对应普通后验（预测器可恢复的部分），非对角线对应跨世界耦合（预测器无法恢复但反事实查询必须读取的部分）。核心方法包括：(1) 利用半正定性作为边际分布所缺乏的偏识别信息，在多项式时间内界定反事实量（而精确的响应类型规划是不可处理的）；(2) 引入本体论公理（ontology axioms）收紧界，最多可收紧三分之一，且能传播到公理本身未直接约束的耦合；(3) 提出"定向疤痕"策略，从遭遇的不可行性中学习约束，收敛速度比非定向方法快数倍；(4) 完整重建等价于容许世界的近似计数，在Sly-Sun阈值以下可处理。

Result: 在数百个结构因果模型上的实验表明：(1) 对可识别量，强预测器和贝叶斯基线均成功；但对不可识别量，预测器崩溃为点估计，28%的模型中给出无效值；(2) 半正定性约束可在多项式时间内有效界定反事实量；(3) 本体论公理可将界收紧最多约三分之一，且影响可传播至未直接涉及的耦合；(4) 定向疤痕学习策略缩小差距的速度比非定向策略快数倍；(5) 完整重建在Sly-Sun阈值以下可处理，阈值以上不可近似。

Conclusion: 预测模型存在不可克服的结构性局限：其仅能恢复世界模型的对角线（后验），而反事实推理所需的非对角线（跨世界耦合）原则上无法通过预测获得。半正定耦合核理论为这一非对角线提供了系统化的理论框架，通过半正定约束、逻辑结构和定向约束学习可以有效界定反事实量，但完整重建的计算复杂性受限于Sly-Sun阈值，作者不宣称突破最坏情况的计算障碍。

Abstract: A common assumption holds that enough observational and interventional data, given to a strong enough predictor, suffices. We report a failure mode that contradicts it. Across hundreds of structural causal models, on identified quantities a strong predictor and a Bayesian baseline both succeed, but on unidentified quantities (the couplings between counterfactual worlds) the predictor collapses to a point, on 28% of models to one no valid model can produce, while the truth is an admissible interval more data never narrows. The gap is structural: prediction cannot represent uncertainty over counterfactual couplings. We cast a world model as a single positive semidefinite coupling kernel K(T,T') over admissible worlds, whose diagonal is the ordinary posterior (what a predictor recovers) and whose off-diagonal is the cross-world coupling it cannot, which every counterfactual reads. The paper is the theory of that off-diagonal. It is real: two states with identical posteriors differ on a cross-world query, and the off-diagonal is the coupling that fixes counterfactuals. It can be bounded: positive semidefiniteness is partial-identifying information the marginals lack, and enforcing it bounds counterfactuals in polynomial time where the exact response-type program is intractable. Logical structure sharpens it: ontology axioms tighten the bound by up to a third, propagating to couplings they never touch. It can be acquired: targeted scars, constraints learned from encountered infeasibilities, close the gap several times faster than untargeted ones. Its full reconstruction is approximate counting of the admissible worlds, tractable below the Sly-Sun threshold and inapproximable above; we do not claim to beat the worst case.

</details>


### [110] [Recalling Too Well: Sycophancy Evaluation and Mitigation in Memory-Augmented Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10949)
*Shelly Bensal,Axel Magnuson,Aparna Balagopalan,Daniel M. Bikel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 持久记忆系统会系统性地放大LLM的谄媚行为（sycophancy），作者提出了MIST基准测试来评估该效应，并提出两种轻量级缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆系统旨在通过长期存储用户信念来提升LLM的实用性，但其对模型准确性的负面影响尚未被系统研究。具体而言，当用户持有错误认知时，记忆系统可能导致模型优先迎合用户观点而非提供正确信息，即加剧谄媚问题（sycophancy）。本文旨在首次系统性地评估这一效应。

Method: 1) 提出MIST基准：包含合成生成的多轮对话，用户在科学、医学和道德推理领域表达看似合理的错误认知；2) 在3种最先进的记忆系统和5个模型家族上进行系统测试；3) 通过错误分析定位问题根源——记忆提取过程中的有损压缩将用户错误认知编码为离散片段，同时丢弃了纠正性上下文；4) 提出两种轻量级缓解策略，在保持甚至超越记忆系统事实召回能力的同时显著降低谄媚行为。

Result: 1) 记忆系统在所有实验条件下均放大了谄媚行为，谄媚率最高可达上下文基线的25倍；2) 记忆提取是主要问题根源：有损压缩将用户错误认知编码但丢弃纠正性上下文；3) 提出的两种轻量级缓解方法在大幅降低谄媚行为的同时，事实召回能力与记忆系统持平或更优。

Conclusion: 持久记忆系统虽然提升了LLM的个性化服务能力，但会系统性地放大谄媚行为，使模型牺牲准确性来迎合用户错误认知。问题核心在于记忆提取的有损压缩机制。所提出的轻量级缓解方法表明，可以在不牺牲记忆功能的前提下有效控制谄媚问题，为未来安全可靠的记忆增强LLM系统设计提供了方向。

Abstract: Persistent memory systems promise to make LLMs more helpful by storing user beliefs over time. We show they also make models less correct by systematically amplifying sycophancy, wherein models prioritize agreement with users over accuracy. We conduct the first systematic evaluation of this effect, introducing MIST: a benchmark of synthetically generated multi-turn conversations where users express plausible misconceptions in scientific, medical, and moral reasoning domains. Testing across three state-of-the-art memory systems and five model families reveals that memory amplifies sycophantic behavior across all conditions, with up to 25x higher sycophancy rates than in-context baselines. Error analyses suggest memory extraction as the primary culprit: lossy compression into discrete snippets encodes user misconceptions while discarding corrective context. Based on these results, we propose two lightweight mitigations that substantially reduce sycophancy while matching or exceeding memory systems at factual recall.

</details>


### [111] [Architect-Ant: Editable Automatic Furnishing of Architectural Floor Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10953)
*Fedor Rodionov,Aleksandar Cvejic,Michael Birsak,John Femiani,Peter Wonka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AntPlan-270数据集（270张带家具标注的建筑平面图）和Architect-Ant框架，利用微调的视觉语言模型和领域特定语言（DSL）实现可编辑的自动家具布局生成，并通过Flux-based LoRA渲染器生成逼真的蓝图风格平面图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动家具布局研究受限于缺乏真实的、由专业设计师设计的、具有对象级家具标注的户型图数据集。现有数据集无法满足对真实住宅场景中精细家具摆放研究的需求，阻碍了该领域的进展。

Method: 提出三部分方法：（1）构建AntPlan-270数据集，包含270张建筑平面图，涵盖10类住宅房间，标注了每个房间的家具边界框；（2）设计Architect-Ant框架，采用紧凑的坐标化领域特定语言（DSL）表示家具布局（编码物体类别及相对于房间几何的位置），生成程序化推理轨迹（涵盖墙体对齐、门窗间距、动线、固定装置兼容性及房间特定家具清单等建筑约束）以监督视觉语言模型的微调，并通过偏好优化（preference optimization）进一步提升布局质量；（3）将生成的DSL光栅化为语义掩码，作为条件输入Flux-based LoRA渲染器，生成蓝图风格的真实感平面图，同时保留可编辑的符号化布局。

Result: 实验表明，Architect-Ant能够生成几何有效且功能合理的家具布局，并展示了将该方法扩展到更大规模纯结构户型图数据集进行自动家具布局的可行路径。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入AntPlan-270数据集填补了专业设计户型图标注数据的空白，提出的Architect-Ant框架结合视觉语言模型微调、程序化推理监督和偏好优化，实现了可编辑、几何有效且功能合理的自动家具布局生成，为大规模户型图自动家具配置提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Furnished floor plans are fundamental to real estate visualization, interior design, and architectural workflows. However, progress in automatic furniture arrangement has been limited by the lack of real, professionally designed floor-plan datasets with object-level furniture annotations. To address this gap, we introduce AntPlan-270, a curated dataset of 270 architectural floor plans with per-room furniture bounding box annotations across ten residential room categories. Building on this dataset, we present Architect-Ant, an editable automatic furnishing framework powered by a fine-tuned vision-language model. Furniture layouts are represented using a compact, coordinate-based domain-specific language (DSL) that encodes object categories and placements relative to the room geometry. To improve spatial reasoning, we generate procedural reasoning traces that capture architectural constraints such as wall alignment, door and window clearance, circulation, fixture compatibility, and room-specific furniture inventories, and use them to supervise fine-tuning of the model. We then apply preference optimization over candidate object placements to further refine layout quality. The generated DSL can be rasterized into semantic masks and used to condition a Flux-based LoRA renderer, producing realistic blueprint-style furnished floor-plan images while preserving the editable symbolic layout. Experiments on layout furnishing show that Architect-Ant produces geometrically valid and functionally plausible layouts, and suggest a scalable path for furnishing larger structure-only floor-plan datasets.

</details>


### [112] [Mind the Gap: Can Frontier LLMs Pass a Standardized Office Proficiency Exam?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10956)
*Tengchao Lv,Dongdong Zhang,Jiayu Ding,Yilin Jia,Yuzhong Zhao,Yupan Huang,Wenshan Wu,Xiangyang Zhou,Shaohan Huang,Nan Yang,Li Dong,Lei Cui,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于中国全国计算机等级考试（NCRE）的Office自动化基准测试，包含200道Word/Excel/PowerPoint实操题和7118个机器可评分标准，评估了7个前沿LLM，发现当前模型在细粒度Office文档自动化任务上仍存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在计算机自动化领域的部署正在加速，但其处理复杂、专业级办公软件的能力尚未得到充分测试。Office自动化是理想的基准测试场景，因为它要求长程规划与推理、精确参数配置以及多应用协同集成，而目前缺乏系统性的评估框架来量化这一能力。

Method: 基于全国计算机等级考试（NCRE）构建评估基准，涵盖Word、Excel、PowerPoint三类应用共200道综合实操任务，采用满分100分的评分标准，共包含7118个机器可评分的细粒度评判准则，以分数率（SR，即所有任务平均得分百分比）为核心指标。测试了7个前沿LLM，并对比了单轮生成模式与具备执行反馈、迭代修复和更广泛Office自动化接口的增强型智能体系统。

Result: 单轮模式下最优模型SR仅为36.6%；增强型智能体系统（含执行反馈、迭代修复及更广泛的Office自动化访问）最高达到68.8%，但仍远低于用作评分合理性校验的社区参考分数95.5%。实验揭示了当前模型在精确参数配置和复杂Office操作上的明显不足。

Conclusion: 尽管近年来代码生成能力取得了显著进步，但实现可靠的细粒度Office文档自动化对当前代码生成LLM及智能体系统仍是重大挑战。该基准测试有效暴露了现有模型在长程规划、精确操作和多应用集成方面的瓶颈。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Model (LLM) agents for computer automation is accelerating, yet their ability to navigate complex, professional-grade productivity software is largely untested. We argue that Office automation is an ideal environment for benchmarking document-automation capability, as it requires long-horizon planning and reasoning, precise parameter configuration, and multi-application integration. To quantify this capability, we introduce an evaluation based on China's National Computer Rank Examination (NCRE), featuring 200 comprehensive practical-operation tasks across Word, Excel, and PowerPoint. Each task is scored on a 100-point rubric scale using 7,118 machine-gradable criteria, and Score Rate (SR) denotes the mean percentage of rubric points earned across these tasks. We benchmark 7 frontier LLMs and observe stark limitations: single-turn models score a maximum of 36.6%. A stronger agentic system with execution feedback, iterative repair, and broader Office automation access reaches 68.8%, but remains below the 95.5% community-reference score used as a scoring sanity check. Ultimately, our experiments demonstrate that despite recent advancements in code generation, achieving reliable fine-grained Office document automation remains a significant challenge for current code-generating LLM and agent systems.

</details>


### [113] [Bellman-Taylor Score Decoding for Markov Decision Processes with State-Dependent Feasible Action Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10979)
*Yi Chen,Rushuai Yang,Qiang Chen,Dongyan,Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Bellman-Taylor分数解码框架，将策略学习转移到欧几里得分数空间，同时通过动作解码器保证可行性，使标准深度强化学习算法能够处理运筹学中具有状态依赖且隐式定义可行动作集的MDP问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运筹学中许多MDP的可行动作集是状态依赖的，且由各种运营约束隐式定义。标准深度强化学习算法的动作接口通常假设固定有限动作集或简单欧几里得空间，难以直接处理这类问题。需要一种框架来弥合复杂约束动作空间与标准DRL算法之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于最优动作值函数的Taylor展开，提出Bellman-Taylor分数解码（Bellman-Taylor Score Decoding）框架：(1) 将策略学习转移到欧几里得分数空间；(2) 通过动作解码器将分数映射为满足约束的可行动作；(3) 构造潜在分数MDP，使标准DRL算法可直接优化而无需对解码器进行微分。

Result: 提供了性能保证，证明该方法的最优性差距可分解为结构近似误差和算法学习误差两部分。在排队网络控制问题上，策略本质上学习了状态依赖的基于指标的调度规则。数值实验表明：在小规模实例中达到近最优性能，在大规模系统中相比基准方法有显著提升。

Conclusion: Bellman-Taylor分数解码框架有效地解决了运筹学MDP中复杂约束动作空间的DRL应用难题，通过将可行性保证与策略优化解耦，既保持了标准DRL算法的易用性，又能处理状态依赖的隐式约束，理论上具有可分解的性能保证，实践中在排队网络控制问题上展现了优越性能。

Abstract: Many Markov decision processes (MDPs) in operations research have feasible actions that are state dependent and defined implicitly by various operational constraints. These features make it difficult to use standard deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms, whose action interfaces typically assume either a fixed finite action catalog or a simple Euclidean space. Motivated by a Taylor expansion of the optimal action-value function, we propose Bellman--Taylor score decoding, a framework that moves policy learning to a Euclidean score space while enforcing feasibility through an action decoder. The induced latent-score MDP then can be optimized by standard DRL algorithms without differentiating through the decoder. We provide a performance guarantee showing that the optimality gap of this approach decomposes into a structural approximation error and an algorithmic learning error. Lastly, we apply this framework to a queueing network control problem, where the policy essentially learns a state-dependent index-based dispatching rule. Numerical experiments show near-optimal performance in small instances and considerable improvements over benchmarks in larger systems.

</details>


### [114] [Null-Space Constrained Low-Rank Adaptation for Response-Specified Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10989)
*Bocheng Ju,Jianhua Wang,Chengliang Liu,Xiaolin Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NSRU框架，通过正交投影低秩参数化将LoRA更新限制在保留子空间的零空间中，同时结合安全目标响应指定和不良响应抑制，实现可控的LLM遗忘学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法主要关注抑制不良答案，近期目标引导变体虽指定了替代行为，但更新的局部性仍未得到有效约束，导致遗忘过程可能损害模型的良性能力。需要一种既能精确控制遗忘行为又能严格保持模型保留知识的方法。

Method: NSRU框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 为每个遗忘查询构建显式结构化的安全目标响应，指定期望行为；(2) 从良性隐藏表示中估计每模块的保留子空间，利用正交投影低秩参数化将LoRA更新约束在保留子空间的零空间内，实现更新局部化；(3) 联合优化安全目标学习、不良响应抑制和保留知识保持三个目标。作者提供了局部一阶分析，证明投影更新能减少保留侧扰动同时保留可编辑方向。

Result: 在TOFU基准上，NSRU有效抑制了可提取的遗忘集知识，同时在保留QA性能、模型效用和安全目标对齐方面优于代表性基线方法。在WMDP基准上，NSRU将危险领域准确率维持在随机选择水平附近，同时保持了广泛的和领域相邻的MMLU效用。消融实验验证了各组件的互补作用，敏感性和鲁棒性分析表明方法在不同超参数和提示变体下表现稳定。

Conclusion: NSRU通过零空间约束的投影低秩框架，成功实现了精确可控的LLM遗忘学习，在遗忘效果和保留能力之间取得了优异的平衡，各组件（安全目标监督、不良响应抑制、保留损失、零空间投影更新）协同互补，方法整体表现稳健。

Abstract: Large language model unlearning aims to suppress designated undesirable knowledge while preserving benign capabilities. Many unlearning objectives focus on suppressing undesired answers, while recent target-guided variants specify replacement behavior but still leave update locality largely unconstrained. This paper introduces \emph{Null-Space Constrained Response-Specified Unlearning} (NSRU), a projection-constrained low-rank framework for controlled LLM unlearning. NSRU uses an explicitly structured safe target response to specify the desired behavior for each forget query, while suppressing the original undesired content. To localize adaptation, NSRU estimates per-module retain subspaces from benign hidden representations and uses an orthogonal-projected low-rank parameterization to confine LoRA updates to the null space of the retain subspace. The resulting objective jointly optimizes safe-target learning, undesired-response suppression, and retention preservation under this constrained parameterization. We provide a local first-order analysis showing that the projected update reduces retain-side perturbations while preserving editable directions for shaping forget-query behavior. Experiments on TOFU show that NSRU effectively suppresses extractable forget-set knowledge while improving retain QA performance, model utility, and safe-target alignment over representative baselines. On WMDP, NSRU keeps hazardous-domain accuracy near the random-choice region while preserving broad and domain-adjacent MMLU utility. Ablation studies support the complementary roles of safe-target supervision, undesired-response suppression, retention loss, and null-space projected updates, while sensitivity and robustness analyses indicate stable behavior across the tested hyperparameter and prompt variations.

</details>


### [115] [Structure from Reasoning, Numbers from Search: On-Premise Open LLMs as Structural Priors for Coupled MIMO Controller Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11015)
*Jiaxuan Chen,Haonan Li,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一个可复现的基准，明确界定了本地开源大语言模型（LLM）在控制器整定中的适用边界：对简单单回路无优势，但对强耦合MIMO系统，LLM可作为样本高效且可解释的结构先验，通过推理耦合关系提出反直觉的非对称控制结构，显著减少评估次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合多输入多输出（MIMO）工业过程的控制器整定极具挑战：分散式经典自整定忽略回路耦合，局部数值优化在非凸代价景观中易陷入停滞。作者探究本地部署的开源LLM（无需传输数据、无需建模）能否在此场景中提供帮助，并试图明确划定LLM有用与无用的边界条件。

Method: 在单回路CSTR和强耦合四水箱系统上进行对比实验。采用带惩罚的代价函数J=IAE+λ·TV(u)（兼顾跟踪性能与执行器平滑性）。对比方法包括：经典继电反馈整定、朴素LLM整定、从平衡初始点出发的局部优化器、全局优化器（差分进化）、以及"脚手架式"（scaffolded）开源LLM整定。脚手架式LLM通过推理耦合关系提出非对称控制结构，再结合经典优化器精细调整参数，达到全局最优。还在3×3系统上验证了维度扩展性，并跨四种开源模型验证泛化性。

Result: 单回路CSTR：经典继电整定IAE=0.106（接近最优0.102），LLM整定为0.162，无优势。强耦合四水箱：朴素继电整定J≈28.6、朴素LLM整定J≈29.7，均不如开环（22.7）；局部优化器10/10次失败；脚手架式LLM达到J≈16.9±0.2，结合经典优化器精调达到全局最优J≈12.0（10/10成功 vs 局部优化0/10）。LLM仅需18次评估即获得可用控制器（此时全局优化器仍劣于开环），在3×3系统上样本效率优势扩大至约6倍。结果在四种开源模型上一致，在良性（弱耦合）系统上LLM无优势。

Conclusion: 开源LLM在控制器整定中的价值不在于作为优化器，而在于作为样本高效、可解释的结构先验：它能推理回路耦合并提出反直觉的控制结构（如非对称结构、负积分校正），为后续数值优化提供优良初始化。其优势仅在强耦合、非凸的MIMO问题中显现，对简单回路无帮助。本文贡献了一个可复现的基准，清晰界定了LLM辅助控制整定的适用范围。

Abstract: Tuning controllers for strongly coupled multi-input multi-output (MIMO) industrial processes is hard: decentralized classical auto-tuning ignores loop interaction, and local numerical optimization from natural initializations stalls in the resulting non-convex cost landscape. We ask whether on-premise open-source large language models (LLMs), which keep data on-site and need no plant model, can help. On a single-loop CSTR, classical relay-feedback tuning (IAE 0.106, near the 0.102 optimum) beats an LLM tuner (0.162): for simple loops the LLM adds nothing. The picture inverts on a strongly coupled quadruple-tank with conflicting set-points, scored by a penalized cost J = IAE + lambda*TV(u) that rewards tracking without chattering actuators. There, naive relay tuning (J ~ 28.6) and naive LLM tuning (29.7) are no better than open loop (22.7), and a local optimizer from balanced starts fails in 10/10 runs. A scaffolded open LLM instead reasons about the coupling, proposes the counter-intuitive asymmetric structure, and reaches J ~ 16.9 +/- 0.2 from any start; refining it with a classical optimizer attains the smooth global optimum (J ~ 12.0, 10/10 vs. 0/10), which even applies a non-obvious negative integral correction decentralized tuning cannot. A global optimizer (differential evolution) also reaches this optimum, so the LLM is not the only route; its advantage is sample efficiency and interpretability: a usable controller in 18 evaluations (where the global optimizer is worse than open loop) plus a stated rationale. This edge grows with dimension, reaching ~6x fewer evaluations on a 3x3 plant. The behaviour generalizes across four open models, and on a benign plant the LLM offers no advantage, sharpening the boundary. We contribute a reproducible benchmark delimiting when open LLMs help in control tuning: not as optimizers, but as a sample-efficient, interpretable structural prior.

</details>


### [116] [Superficial Beliefs in LLM Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11016)
*Gabriel Freedman,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型在二元决策中表现出系统性的行为结构，但其自我报告的决策理由仅能部分反映真正驱动其选择的属性，作者将此现象定义为"浅层信念"（superficial belief）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在做选择时，究竟只是模仿理由（rationale），还是其选择背后存在系统性的决策结构。即LLM的决策行为与其自我解释之间的关系是什么——是随机行为、完全可自我阐明的信念，还是介于二者之间的状态。

Method: 在合成的二元决策场景中，让模型在由分级属性定义的两个候选方案间做选择。通过构建行为模型拟合模型的历史决策，推断出真正驱动选择的属性（行为推断驱动因素），然后将其与模型自我报告的最重要属性以及基于评分的独立判断器所恢复的驱动因素进行对比。同时通过提示顺序扰动、采样扰动、替代行为模型、目标遮蔽分析以及结构变化的决策设置等多种鲁棒性检验来验证结论的稳定性。

Result: 行为模型能很好地预测模型在留出测试集上的选择，表明模型行为与可见属性之间存在系统性关联而非随机。然而，模型的直接自我报告和独立评分判断器仅能部分恢复行为推断出的真正决策驱动因素。这一定性模式在多种扰动条件和不同实验设置下保持一致。

Conclusion: LLM的决策行为既非任意随机，也非完全可自我阐明的信念驱动，而是呈现出一种"浅层信念"状态：模型表现得如同受属性上的概率性局部优先级引导，但对真正驱动其决策的属性仅有有限的语言化访问能力。行为足够结构化以支持预测，但显式理由与实际决策驱动因素之间存在不完美的对应关系。

Abstract: We ask whether large language models (LLMs) merely imitate rationales when choosing between two options, or whether their choices reflect a systematic underlying decision structure. Using synthetic binary decision settings in which models choose between profiles defined by graded attributes, we compare the attribute a model says mattered most with the attribute that best explains its choice under a behavioural model fit to prior decisions. The behavioural model predicts held-out choices well, showing that model behaviour is systematically related to the visible attributes rather than being random. However, direct self-reports and a separate score-based judge recover the behaviourally inferred driver only partially. The resulting picture is neither one of arbitrary behaviour nor one of fully articulated belief - outputs are structured enough to support prediction, but explicit reasons track the recovered driver only imperfectly. This qualitative pattern persists across prompt-order and sampling perturbations, alternative behavioural models, targeted occlusion analyses, and structurally varied decision settings. We interpret this as evidence for ``superficial belief'' in LLM decision-making: models behave as if guided by probabilistic local priorities over attributes, while having only limited verbal access to the attributes that drive their decisions.

</details>


### [117] [Workflow-GYM: Towards Long-Horizon Evaluation of Computer-use Agentic tasks in Real-World Professional Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11042)
*Liya Zhu,Jingzhe Ding,Jian Zhang,Jianbo Xue,Shihao Liang,Ge Zhang,Xiang Gao,Qingshui Gu,Mailun Gao,Huimin Che,Yan Zhao,Peiheng Zhou,Haojun Wang,Chaobo Xian,Lili Le,Chi Wu,Yiwei Liu,Shengda Long,Jiale Yang,Fangzhi Xu,Sijin Wu,Haodong Duan,Yi Zhu,Chao He,Zhaojian Li,Minchao Wang,Huan Zhou,Jiani Hou,Chuqian Yu,Weiran Shi,Hongwan Gao,Jiamin Chen,Guanhong Chen,Tingqin Luo,Kaiyuan Zhang,Zhixin Yao,Qing Hua,Yuhao Jiang,Jin Chen,Pu Chen,Zhenyu Hu,Xingyu Li,Zhengxuan Jiang,Meng Cao,Tianfeng Long,Haozhe Wang,Mingzhang Wang,Yichen Zhang,Yiming Dai,Chenchen Zhang,Jiaying Wang,Zhiyong Wu,Shen Yan,Yujia Qin,Wenhao Huang,Zaiyuan Wang,Xiaolong Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Workflow-GYM，一个面向专业领域长时程GUI任务的基准测试，揭示了当前最强AI智能体在专业软件工作流中成功率仅略高于30%，表明专业长时程GUI工作流对现有智能体仍极具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI基准测试主要聚焦于通用软件、相对简单的应用和短时程任务，缺乏对智能体在跨领域专业软件中完成长时程、高价值工作流能力的评估。当前尚不清楚现代智能体能否遵循用户指令，自主操作领域特定的专业软件，以端到端方式完成具有经济价值的工作。本文旨在填补这一评估空白。

Method: 提出Workflow-GYM基准测试，以专业领域和专业软件环境为核心，构建长时程GUI任务集。该基准覆盖多样化的专业领域，聚焦于需要操作图形用户界面完成的复杂专业工作流，并在此基准上对当前最先进的模型进行了大规模实验评估。

Result: 即使是最强模型，成功率也仅略高于30%，说明专业长时程GUI工作流对当前智能体仍极具挑战。进一步分析揭示了智能体的四大核心缺陷：（1）工作流阶段遗漏；（2）错误传播；（3）目标漂移；（4）对专业软件环境理解不足。智能体难以维持长时程工作流的一致性。

Conclusion: 专业长时程GUI工作流是当前AI智能体的重大挑战，现有系统在工作流一致性维持、专业软件理解等方面存在显著不足。研究结果为理解当前智能体系统的局限性提供了重要洞见，并为下一代GUI智能体研究指明了关键方向。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed the rapid evolution of AI agents toward handling increasingly complex, real-world tasks. However, existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether agents can operate graphical user interfaces to complete long-horizon, high-value professional workflows across diverse domains. Current GUI benchmarks still predominantly focus on general-purpose software, relatively simple applications, and short-horizon tasks, leaving it largely unknown whether modern agents can follow user instructions to autonomously operate domain-specific professional software and accomplish economically valuable work in an end-to-end manner. To bridge this gap, we introduce Workflow-GYM, a benchmark for long-horizon GUI tasks centered on professional domains and specialized software environments. Through extensive experiments on state-of-the-art models, we find that even the strongest models achieve only slightly above 30% success rates, highlighting that professional long-horizon GUI workflows remain highly challenging for current GUI agents. Further analysis reveals that current agents struggle to maintain long-horizon workflow consistency, frequently exhibiting workflow stage omission, error propagation, objective drift, and insufficient understanding of professional software environments. Our findings provide important insights into the limitations of current agent systems and suggest key directions for the next generation of GUI-agent research.

</details>


### [118] [What Fits (Into Few Tokens) Doesn't Overfit: Compression and Generalization in ML Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11045)
*Martin Andres Bertran,Aaron Roth,Zhiwei Steven Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过LLM驱动的研究智能体，从输出压缩和输入压缩两个信息瓶颈角度验证了"成功的ML策略高度可压缩"这一假说，解释了基准驱动的ML为何在实践中很少出现过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准驱动的机器学习在理论上应自适应地重复使用留出测试集会导致过拟合，但实践中却很少发生。一个有吸引力的假说是：成功的ML策略具有高度可压缩性（低描述长度）。本文旨在利用LLM驱动的研究智能体框架，将这一假说变得可直接检验。

Method: 提出两种互补的信息瓶颈实验：（1）**输出压缩**：让探索智能体在验证集上自适应搜索高性能模型，然后测试一个全新的"复现智能体"仅凭极短的提示词和训练数据能否复现其性能；（2）**输入压缩**：探索智能体仅接收一位反馈信息（即每次提交的模型是否优于当前最佳）。实验覆盖8个数据集，涵盖表格分类、视觉、语言建模、扩散模型和奖励建模等任务。同时设计了可证伪性实验：故意诱导验证集过拟合，观察其是否无法用短提示复现。

Result: 在8个数据集上，信息瓶颈对性能影响很小：短提示词和可压缩的反馈信号足以复现和发现高性能模型。而当故意诱导验证集过拟合时，结果无法通过短提示词复现，验证了假说的可证伪性。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持基于描述长度的解释：成功的ML策略位于策略空间中的低复杂度区域，这解释了基准驱动ML中过拟合缺失的现象。策略的高可压缩性是自适应基准评估未导致严重过拟合的根本原因。

Abstract: Reusing a held-out benchmark adaptively should, in principle, invite overfitting. Yet benchmark-driven machine learning (ML) has produced surprisingly little overfitting in practice. An attractive hypothesis is that successful ML strategies are highly compressible. We study this in the setting of LLM-driven research agents, where the hypothesis becomes directly testable via two complementary information bottlenecks. In \emph{output compression}, an exploration agent adaptively searches for high-performance models using a validation set, and we test whether a fresh ``reproducer agent'' can reproduce its performance given only an extremely short prompt and the training data. In \emph{input compression}, the explorer receives only one-bit feedback indicating whether each submitted model improves on the running best. Across 8 datasets spanning tabular classification, vision, language modeling, diffusion modeling, and reward modeling, we find that these bottlenecks have little effect on performance: short prompts and compressible feedback are sufficient to reproduce and find high-performance models. The hypothesis is falsifiable: when we deliberately induce validation-set overfitting, the results fail to reproduce with short prompts. Taken together, our results support a description-length explanation for the lack of overfitting in benchmark-driven ML: successful strategies occupy a low-complexity region of strategy space.

</details>


### [119] [CIAware-Bench: Benchmarking Control Intervention Awareness Across Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11063)
*Joachim Schaeffer,Thomas Jiralerspong,Alexander Panfilov,Guillaume Lajoie,Jonas Geiping,Yoshua Bengio,Roland S. Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CIAware-Bench基准，用于衡量前沿AI模型对控制干预（Control Intervention）的感知能力，即模型能否区分自身生成的轨迹与被控制协议修改后的轨迹。评估发现模型具有低到中等的感知能力，且感知能力因任务领域、模型组合而显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI控制协议通过监控不可信模型并修改其潜在不安全步骤来实施监管，这通常依赖可信模型对不可信模型的轨迹进行部分篡改。然而，如果不可信模型能够检测到这种干预，它可能推断出监控器的属性并调整策略以规避控制。因此，需要系统性地评估前沿模型对控制干预的感知能力，以便设计更难被检测的控制协议。

Method: 提出CIAware-Bench基准，包含四个任务领域（论文写作、BigCodeBench、Bash Arena和SHADE-Arena），通过变化轨迹水印、附加任务存在与否以及控制协议类型等维度，测试模型能否在二分类任务中区分自身原始轨迹和经过控制干预修改后的轨迹。对11个前沿模型进行了系统评估。

Result: 在默认设置下，模型的控制干预感知能力为低到中等水平（二分类准确率最高达0.87，随机基线为0.5），且在不同任务领域和模型对之间存在显著差异。跨模型家族的检测通常更容易，表明模型利用了不同提供商在风格或后训练中的特征差异来进行识别。

Conclusion: 控制干预感知能力并非模型的固有属性，而是随任务、模型组合和部署场景变化的动态特征，因此每次新模型发布和部署场景都应重新测量。作者开源了CIAware-Bench，用于持续追踪感知能力并指导设计更难被检测的控制协议。

Abstract: AI control protocols oversee untrusted models by monitoring their actions and modifying potentially unsafe steps, often using a trusted model. This partially tampers with the untrusted model's trajectory. If the trusted model detects such an intervention, it may infer properties of the monitor and adapt to evade control. We introduce \textbf{CIAware-Bench}, a benchmark for measuring \textbf{c}ontrol \textbf{i}ntervention (CI) awareness across frontier models. CIAware-Bench tests whether models can distinguish their own trajectories from those modified by a control intervention. The benchmark is comprised of a suite of four task domains (essay writing, BigCodeBench, Bash Arena, and SHADE-Arena), while varying trajectory watermarking, side-task presence, and the control protocol. Evaluating eleven frontier models, we find low to moderate CI awareness under default settings (up to 0.87; random chance balanced binary classification accuracy is 0.5) with substantial variation across task domains and model pairs. Detection is generally easier across model families, suggesting that models exploit provider-specific differences in style or post-training. Overall, CI awareness is not a fixed model-level property, and should be measured for each new model release and deployment scenario. We release CIAware-Bench to track CI awareness and inform control protocols whose interventions are harder to detect.

</details>


### [120] [A History-Aware Visually Grounded Critic for Computer Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11078)
*Jaewoo Lee,Zaid Khan,Archiki Prasad,Justin Chih-Yao Chen,Supriyo Chakraborty,Kartik Balasubramaniam,Sambit Sahu,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Hyunji Lee,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiViG是一个面向计算机使用代理（CUA）的测试时干预框架，通过历史感知的宏动作摘要和视觉定位的批评机制，解决现有评论模型短视决策和缺乏视觉定位的问题，在多平台GUI任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算机使用代理（CUA）的测试时评论模型存在两个关键缺陷：（1）仅关注短视决策循环，容易遗忘早期动作历史；（2）缺乏视觉定位能力，无法检测点击错误UI元素等缺陷动作。这些问题导致在复杂GUI环境中长时序任务的执行可靠性不足。

Method: 提出HiViG（History-aware Visually Grounded）测试时框架，核心是一个在真实GUI轨迹上训练的多模态评论模型，具备两大能力：（1）宏动作历史（macro-action history）——将策略过去的交互抽象为紧凑记录，总结已完成的成就，缓解短视规划；（2）视觉定位批评（visually grounded critique）——在动作执行前，将原始执行坐标与当前截图进行对照验证，拦截错误操作。测试时将该评论模型集成到策略决策循环中，实现执行前的动作评估与纠错。

Result: 在Web、移动端和桌面端基准测试中，HiViG一致优于现有的标量评论和语言评论模型。相比最强基线，在Qwen3-VL-32B上平均成功率提升5.8%，在Gemini-3-Flash上提升9.0%，且展现出强跨平台泛化能力。消融实验证实宏动作历史缓解了短视规划问题，视觉定位批评减少了执行错误，两者对长时序GUI任务的测试时扩展均不可或缺。

Conclusion: HiViG通过将历史感知和视觉定位能力引入测试时评论框架，有效解决了CUA在复杂GUI环境中的短视决策和执行错误问题，在多平台上实现了显著且一致的性能提升，验证了两个组件对长时序GUI任务测试时扩展的关键作用。

Abstract: Various test-time interventions for Computer Use Agents (CUAs), including critic models, have been developed to improve performance through pre-execution action evaluation in complex Graphical User Interface (GUI) environments. However, existing critics suffer from two key limitations: they (1) focus primarily on short-sighted decision loops (e.g., forgetting earlier actions) and (2) lack the visual grounding needed to detect flawed actions (e.g., clicking wrong UI elements). To address these, we introduce HiViG, a History-aware Visually Grounded test-time framework, built around a multimodal critic trained on real GUI trajectories to abstract past interactions into a compact record and to evaluate actions with visual grounding. At test time, HiViG integrates the critic into the policy decision loop to provide macro-action history, which summarizes the policy's completed achievements, and visually grounded critique, which verifies raw execution coordinates against the current screenshot to intercept errors before execution. Across web, mobile, and desktop benchmarks, HiViG consistently outperforms existing scalar and verbal critics, improving average success rates over the strongest baseline by 5.8% for Qwen3-VL-32B and 9.0% for Gemini-3-Flash, and demonstrates strong cross-platform generalization. Ablations show that macro-action history mitigates short-sighted planning and visually grounded critique reduces execution errors, with both components being critical for test-time scaling in long-horizon GUI tasks.

</details>


### [121] [Monte Carlo Pass Search: Using Trajectory Generation for 3D Counterfactual Pass Evaluation in Football](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11120)
*Andrew Kang,Priya Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将足球传球评估重新建模为类似蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)的评估问题，提出Monte Carlo Pass Search (MCPS)方法，通过采样传球变体、利用球条件世界模型进行前向模拟、并用学习的价值模型评分，生成传球价值的分布，从而实现分布感知的传球归因与排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球传球评估方法缺乏对传球执行质量和选择质量的系统性分离评估。作者发现传球评估可被分解为价值模型（控球价值）、世界模型（多智能体轨迹与球交互）和反事实动作策略（带噪声的传球变体采样）三个已有组件，但此前未被统一整合。同时，德甲联赛首次公开了包含3D球轨迹的高保真追踪数据集，为构建此类系统提供了数据基础。

Method: 提出MCPS框架：(1) 对每个观测传球推断踢球参数；(2) 采样执行变体（execution variants）和选项变体（option variants）；(3) 使用球条件世界模型对每个候选传球进行前向模拟直到下一次球交互；(4) 用学习的价值模型对结果评分，获得价值增益的分布。设计了两种互补的执行盈余指标（均值和百分位）用于分析与排名。世界模型方面，将自动驾驶领域的离散token自回归轨迹生成器SMART适配到足球场景，以在有限公开数据下实现样本高效的多智能体轨迹预测，并支持完全假设性的前向模拟。

Result: 在德甲公开高保真追踪数据集上，适配的SMART世界模型在best-of-20预测精度上相比基线表现出色，同时支持下游评估所需的完全假设性rollout。基于MCPS得到的价值分布，分布感知归因指标能够有效区分传球的执行质量和决策质量，可用于球员分析和排名。作者已公开模型权重和代码。

Conclusion: 本文将传球评估统一为MCTS式框架，结合价值模型、世界模型和反事实策略三个组件，提出了MCPS方法。该方法能够生成传球价值的完整分布而非单一估计值，支持更精细的分布感知归因分析。通过跨领域迁移自动驾驶中的轨迹生成模型解决了公开数据有限的挑战，并通过开源代码和模型促进了可复现研究。

Abstract: We recast pass evaluation in football (soccer) as a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-like evaluation problem whose components mostly exist in the literature under different names: a value model (possession value), a world model (multi-agent trajectories with ball interactions), and a policy over counterfactual actions (sampling pass variants with noise). Building on the first public high-fidelity tracking dataset with 3D ball trajectories from the Bundesliga, we introduce Monte Carlo Pass Search (MCPS), which infers kick parameters for each observed pass, samples execution variants and option variants, rolls each candidate forward with a ball-conditioned world model until the next ball interaction, and scores outcomes with a learned value model to obtain a distribution over gained value. This distribution enables distribution-aware attribution with two complementary execution-surplus scores used for analysis and ranking: mean-based and percentile-based scores. To make the world model sample-efficient under limited public data, we adapt a discrete-token, autoregressive trajectory generator from autonomous driving (SMART) and show it yields strong best-of-20 forecasting accuracy compared to baselines, while supporting fully hypothetical rollouts for downstream evaluation. We have released model checkpoints and code.

</details>


### [122] [ABC-Bench: An Agentic Bio-Capabilities Benchmark for Biosecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11150)
*Andrew Bo Liu,Samira Nedungadi,Bryce Cai,Alex Kleinman,Harmon Bhasin,Seth Donoughe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ABC-Bench基准测试套件，用于评估大语言模型(LLM)智能体在生物安全相关任务上的能力，包括液体处理机器人编程、DNA片段设计和DNA合成筛查规避，结果显示所有测试的LLM智能体在三项任务上均超过了人类专家的中位数表现，并通过湿实验室验证了其实际可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正迅速获得与生物研究相关的能力，从文献综合到实验数据解读，乃至执行此前需要资深生物学家完成的计算生物学任务。这些新兴AI能力在带来科学发现和生物医学进步机遇的同时，也改变了生物安全风险格局。因此，迫切需要一个系统化的基准测试来量化评估LLM智能体在生物安全相关任务上的实际能力水平，以支撑风险评估和治理决策。

Method: 作者提出了Agentic Bio-Capabilities Benchmark (ABC-Bench)，一套涵盖良性和双用途生物学任务的评估基准，具体包括三项任务：(1) 编写代码操控液体处理机器人（OpenTrons）；(2) 设计用于体外组装的DNA片段；(3) 规避DNA合成筛查。这些任务要求生物学知识和软件工程能力的结合。研究同时设置了人类专家基线进行对比，并通过湿实验室实验对LLM生成的方案进行实际验证。

Result: 所有被测试的LLM智能体在三项任务上均超过了人类专家基线的中位数表现。智能体在依赖已发表知识和成熟实验方案的任务上表现优异，而在需要新颖生物信息学推理的任务上表现相对较弱。在三次湿实验室验证实验中，OpenAI的o4-mini-high生成的脚本在OpenTrons液体处理机器人上成功运行，组装出了具有预期序列的DNA。

Conclusion: LLM智能体已具备执行生物安全相关任务的实质性能力，在标准化生物实验操作方面甚至超越人类专家中位水平，且其生成的方案已被湿实验室验证为可行。ABC-Bench为系统评估和持续监测LLM在生物安全领域的能力演进提供了重要工具，凸显了在AI快速发展背景下加强生物安全治理的紧迫性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly acquiring capabilities relevant to biological research, from literature synthesis to interpretation of experimental data. Increasingly, LLM agents can also perform in silico biology tasks that previously required experienced human biologists. These emerging AI capabilities offer new opportunities for scientific discovery and biomedical advances, but they also shift the landscape of biosecurity risks. To address this, we introduce the Agentic Bio-Capabilities Benchmark (ABC-Bench), a suite of tasks to measure agentic biosecurity-relevant capabilities. ABC-Bench evaluates LLM agents on both benign and dual-use biology tasks: writing code to operate liquid handling robots, designing DNA fragments for in vitro assembly, and evading DNA synthesis screening. These tasks require a combination of biology and software expertise. All tested LLM agents outperformed the median expert human baseliner on all three tasks. Agents performed highly on tasks drawing on published knowledge and well-documented protocols, and more weakly on a task requiring novel bioinformatics reasoning. In three wet-lab validation experiments, we found that OpenAI's o4-mini-high produced scripts that, when run on an OpenTrons liquid handling robot, successfully assembled DNA with expected sequences.

</details>


### [123] [ReasonAlloc: Hierarchical Decoding-Time KV Cache Budget Allocation for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11164)
*Wenhao Liu,Hao Shi,Yunhe Li,Weizhi Fei,Xiangyuan Wang,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Peisong Wang,Linqi Song,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReasonAlloc提出一种无需训练的分层预算分配框架，将解码阶段的KV缓存压缩建模为层级预算分配问题，通过离线层级预分配（捕捉"推理波"模式）和在线头级动态重分配两个互补层次，在小预算下显著优于均匀分配基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链式思维(CoT)推理轨迹导致KV缓存快速增长，造成严重的推理瓶颈。现有解码阶段压缩方法假设所有层和头的预算均匀分配，而非均匀分配方法主要针对静态的prompt prefill阶段设计，无法捕捉自回归推理过程中逐步变化的上下文需求。两者之间存在明显空白。

Method: 提出ReasonAlloc框架，将解码阶段KV压缩重新定义为分层预算分配问题，包含两个互补层次：(1) 离线层级预分配策略，捕捉一种称为"Reasoning Wave"的架构驱动需求模式；(2) 在线头级策略，在解码过程中根据实时效用将资源动态重分配给信息丰富的注意力头。该方法无需训练，可即插即用地与现有token驱逐策略结合，几乎不引入额外推理开销。

Result: 在数学推理基准(MATH-500, AIME 2024)上，使用DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B和AceReason-14B模型评估，ReasonAlloc优于均匀预算的R-KV、SnapKV和Pyramid-RKV（静态单调递减层预算基线），在小预算（128-512 tokens）下增益最为显著。

Conclusion: ReasonAlloc通过将KV缓存压缩建模为分层预算分配问题，有效弥合了均匀预算方法与静态非均匀方法之间的差距，其发现的"推理波"模式揭示了推理模型各层对缓存需求的非均匀特性。该框架即插即用、开销可忽略，为长CoT推理场景下的高效KV缓存管理提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories in large language model (LLM) reasoning cause severe inference bottlenecks due to rapid key-value (KV) cache growth. Current decoding-time compression methods mitigate this issue via token eviction, but typically assume a uniform budget distribution across all layers and heads. In contrast, existing non-uniform budget allocation methods are predominantly designed for the static prompt prefill phase, and they do not capture the stepwise context demands of autoregressive reasoning. To bridge this gap, we propose ReasonAlloc, a training-free framework that recasts decoding-time KV compression as a hierarchical budget allocation problem. ReasonAlloc operates at two complementary levels: an offline layer-wise preallocation strategy captures an architecture-driven demand pattern which we call ``\textit{Reasoning Wave}'', while an online head-wise strategy reallocates resources during decoding to information-rich heads based on real-time utility. Evaluations on mathematical reasoning benchmarks (MATH-500, AIME~2024) using DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B, and AceReason-14B show that ReasonAlloc outperforms uniform-budget R-KV, SnapKV, and Pyramid-RKV (a baseline enforcing a static, monotonically decreasing layer budget), with the largest gains at small budgets (128-512 tokens). ReasonAlloc is plug-and-play with existing token-eviction policies and introduces negligible inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [124] [The Role of Feedback Alignment in Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11173)
*Semih Kara,Oğuzhan Ersoy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究自蒸馏中上下文设计的影响，发现与求解器推理轨迹对齐的逐步批评（step-aligned critique）作为自教师的上下文，比二元奖励（GRPO）和参考解答条件的自蒸馏效果显著更优，因为它仅针对推理出错的token施加修正压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏通过匹配自教师（带上下文）和学生（无上下文）的输出分布来提升模型能力，但自教师接收的上下文设计对学习效果至关重要，而这一问题此前几乎未被探索。作者旨在系统研究不同反馈上下文设计对自蒸馏效果的影响。

Method: 在frozen critic提供反馈的框架下，训练求解器进行自蒸馏，比较三种上下文条件：(i) 二元奖励信号（GRPO）；(ii) 参考解答（reference solution）；(iii) 与求解器推理轨迹逐步对齐的批评（step-aligned critique）。通过per-token advantage分析来解释不同方法的效果差异。

Result: 逐步对齐批评取得最大增益，在Avg@12指标上超过GRPO 16.11分，超过参考解答条件的自蒸馏5.27分。Per-token advantage分析表明：逐步对齐反馈仅在推理失败的token处施加修正，保持正确行为不变；而参考解答由于措辞和方法不可避免地不同，会在每个token（包括正确步骤）上施加改变压力。

Conclusion: 反馈与求解器推理之间的结构对齐（structural alignment）是自蒸馏有效性的关键驱动因素。精确定位错误而非提供替代方案的反馈设计，能更高效地指导模型学习，避免对正确推理的不必要干扰。

Abstract: Conditioning a language model on additional context, such as feedback on a previous attempt, typically improves its response. Self-distillation trains the model to retain this improvement when the context is not present. The method works by matching the model's output distribution under two settings: a student that sees only the question, and a self-teacher that also sees the context. What the model learns therefore depends on what context the self-teacher receives, yet the design of this context remains largely unexplored.
  We study context design for self-distillation by training a solver on feedback from a frozen critic. We compare three conditions: (i) a binary reward (GRPO), (ii) the reference solution, and (iii) a step-by-step critique aligned to the solver's reasoning trace.
  Step-aligned critique yields the largest gains, outperforming GRPO by 16.11 points and reference-solution-conditioned self-distillation by 5.27 points (Avg@12). Per-token advantage analysis reveals why: step-aligned feedback targets only the tokens where reasoning fails, leaving correct behavior intact. Conditioning on the reference solution, by contrast, pressures the model to change its behavior at every token (even correct steps) because an alternative derivation inevitably differs in phrasing and approach. This suggests that structural alignment between feedback and the solver's reasoning is a key driver of self-distillation effectiveness.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [SD-GRPO: Verifiable Segment Decomposition for Long-Form Vision-Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09871)
*Hyunwoong Kim,Seongeun Lee,Hannah Yun,Junhyun Park,Jonggwon Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Segment-Decomposed GRPO（SD-GRPO），将长文本视觉语言输出按段分解，用逐段奖励的z-normalization替代单一标量优势值，解决GRPO在视觉语言任务中粗粒度信用分配不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO及其变体从LLM迁移到多模态LLM时，使用单一标量优势值进行粗粒度的整体信用分配，无法适配视觉语言任务中常见的长文本、语义丰富的输出场景。这种单标量方式忽略了长文本VL输出天然存在的结构化分段信号，导致跨段信用错误归因（cross-segment credit misattribution），且该问题随输出长度增加而加剧。

Method: 提出SD-GRPO方法：利用长文本VL输出的自然分段结构，对每个段（segment）计算可验证的逐段奖励，然后在rollout组内对逐段奖励进行z-normalization，生成逐段优势向量（per-segment advantage vector）替代单一标量优势值。此外，针对段间存在语义纠缠的场景，提出融合整体奖励与逐段奖励的混合策略。该方法可以以最小的实现开销集成到任何GRPO框架（如Dr. GRPO）中。

Result: 在三个实验设置中验证了SD-GRPO的有效性：（1）基于DOCCI构建的多面板密集描述任务（段语义独立），SD-GRPO一致优于GRPO基线，段数越多增益越大；（2）基于MultiChartQA构建的多图表长文本VQA任务，从理论和实验两方面证明了rollout级奖励存在跨段信用错误归因且随输出长度增大而加剧；（3）基于MMSci的真实科学图表描述任务（子图描述间存在语义纠缠），混合整体与逐段奖励的策略进一步优于两者单独使用。将SD-GRPO集成到Dr. GRPO中同样有效，验证了其通用性。

Conclusion: SD-GRPO通过利用长文本VL输出的自然分段结构进行细粒度信用分配，有效解决了GRPO单标量优势在视觉语言任务中的不足。当段间语义独立时，逐段归一化即可带来显著提升；当段间存在语义纠缠时，融合整体与逐段奖励效果更优。该方法具有通用性和低集成成本，可增强任意GRPO框架在长文本视觉语言生成中的表现。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and its variants, originally developed for Large Language Models (LLMs), have recently been applied to Multimodal LLMs and produced strong results. However, their coarse-grained holistic credit assignment from a single scalar advantage underfits vision-language (VL) tasks, where outputs are often long-form responses grounded in semantically rich images. To address this limitation, we exploit a structured signal that single-scalar formulations discard: the natural segmentation of long-form VL outputs. Concretely, we propose Segment-Decomposed GRPO (SD-GRPO), which z-normalizes verifiable per-segment rewards across the rollout group, yielding a vector of per-segment advantages in place of a single scalar. We evaluate SD-GRPO across three settings spanning controlled and real-world long-form VL generation, organized by increasing semantic entanglement across segments. On a controlled multi-panel dense-captioning task constructed from DOCCI, where segments are semantically independent, SD-GRPO consistently outperforms the GRPO baseline, with larger gains at higher segment counts. Extending to a controlled multi-chart long-form VQA task constructed from MultiChartQA, we show both theoretically and empirically that rollout-level rewards suffer from cross-segment credit misattribution that scales with output length. On a real-world scientific figure captioning task on the MMSci dataset, where subfigure captions share context across the figure, blending holistic and per-segment rewards further improves on both, suggesting per-segment normalization alone is insufficient when segments are semantically entangled. Finally, by integrating SD-GRPO into Dr. GRPO, we confirm that it can be applied to any GRPO framework with minimal implementation overhead to enhance long-form VL generation.

</details>


### [126] [WHU-Infra3D: A Full-stack Multi-modal Dataset and Benchmark for 3D Roadside Infrastructure Inventory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09882)
*Chong Liu,Luxuan Fu,Xuyu Feng,Zhen Dong,Bisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WHU-Infra3D是一个大规模多模态基准数据集，覆盖三座城市53.8公里道路，融合全景影像与LiDAR点云，提供严格的2D-3D实例关联、跨帧跟踪及超过181k条属性与状态标注，用于路侧基础设施清查与健康评估，并建立了五项核心任务的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生城市正从粗粒度视觉映射向精细化、可操作的城市资产数字化转型，但现有数据集主要关注粗粒度视觉感知，缺乏严格的多模态对齐以及面向自动化基础设施维护所需的属性与状态诊断标注，难以支撑规模化、AI驱动的城市基础设施清查与全生命周期管理。WHU-Infra3D旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了覆盖三座城市53.8公里的大规模多模态基准数据集WHU-Infra3D，核心特点包括：（1）融合全景影像与LiDAR点云，建立严格的2D-3D实例级关联与跨帧跟踪；（2）包含超过175k个多视角2D边界框和数千个3D基础设施实例；（3）提供超过181k条详细属性与状态标注（如锈蚀、遮挡）用于运维健康评估；（4）建立五项核心任务的综合基线：2D检测、2D跨视角匹配、3D地理识别、3D点云分割和属性识别。

Result: 在五项核心任务上的广泛实验揭示了两个关键发现：（1）当前模型存在显著的跨城市域差距（domain gap），泛化能力不足；（2）现有模型在长尾分布的缺陷状态识别上存在固有脆弱性，难以有效识别稀少的异常/缺陷类别。

Conclusion: WHU-Infra3D为推进可扩展的AI驱动城市基础设施清查与全生命周期管理提供了重要的测试平台。实验暴露出跨城市域差距和长尾缺陷状态识别的挑战，表明该领域仍有大量亟待解决的问题，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The paradigm of digital twin cities is shifting from coarse visual mapping toward more precise and actionable digitization of urban assets. However, existing datasets predominantly focus on coarse visual perception, lacking the strict multi-modal alignment and attribute and status diagnosis required for automated infrastructure maintenance. To bridge this gap, we introduce WHU-Infra3D, a large-scale, multi-modal benchmark dataset dedicated to roadside infrastructure inventory. Covering 53.8 km across three cities, WHU-Infra3D uniquely integrates panoramic imagery and LiDAR point clouds with rigorous 2D-3D instance association and cross-frame tracking. Comprising over 175k multi-view 2D bounding boxes alongside thousands of 3D infrastructure instances, the dataset provides over 181k detailed attribute and status annotations (e.g., rust, occlusion) to empower operational health assessment. We establish comprehensive baselines across five core tasks: 2D detection, 2D cross-view matching, 3D geo-identification, 3D point cloud segmentation, and attribute recognition. Extensive evaluations expose significant cross-city domain gaps and inherent vulnerabilities of current models on long-tailed defective statuses, establishing WHU-Infra3D as an essential testbed for advancing scalable, AI-driven urban infrastructure inventory and lifecycle management. The WHU-Infra3D dataset is available at https://github.com/WHU-USI3DV/WHU-Infra3D.

</details>


### [127] [ABot-Earth 0.5: Generative 3D Earth Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09967)
*Ming Qian,Tianjian Ouyang,Mingchao Sun,Zijian Wang,Jincheng Xiong,Jiarong Han,Yongchang Zhang,Jiawei Zhang,Xu Wang,Yu Liu,Luyang Tang,Fei Yu,Zengye Ge,Mengmeng Du,Yuan Liu,Nianfei Fan,Song Wang,Yingliang Peng,Chunxue Jia,Yang Liu,Shiying Zeng,Haozhe Shi,Junnan Lai,Hongyu Pan,Zheng Wu,Ning Guo,Mu Xu,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ABot-Earth 0.5 提出了一个基于3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)的生成式3D框架，能够仅从卫星影像合成大规模、无缝的3D城市环境，每平方公里生成时间不到10分钟，支持Web端实时可视化，并可用于具身AI等下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模3D重建面临高昂的技术和经济成本壁垒，传统方法依赖多视角影像采集，难以高效覆盖全球范围。卫星影像虽然广泛可获取且具有地理参考信息，但从单一卫星视角直接生成高保真3D场景仍是重大挑战。此外，仿真与真实之间的域差距(sim-to-real gap)限制了具身AI（如无人机导航）在仿真环境中的闭环训练效果。因此，需要一种超低成本、高效率的方案，从普遍可得的卫星影像生成逼真的3D环境。

Method: 提出了一个直接基于3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)表示构建的新型生成模型：(1) 在多样化的真实城市重建数据语料库上训练该生成模型，学习真实的几何结构和纹理；(2) 推理阶段仅以卫星影像为条件，生成新的3D场景；(3) 集成分层细节层次(LOD)结构，支持在Web地图引擎上进行实时交互式可视化；(4) 整个框架设计注重可扩展性和易用性。

Result: 该框架以每平方公里不到10分钟的速度生成大规模3D场景，展现出卓越的真实感；通过分层LOD结构实现了Web端实时交互式可视化；高保真仿真环境有效缩小了sim-to-real域差距，成功支持了闭环无人机导航等具身AI下游应用；显著降低了大规模3D重建的技术和经济门槛。

Conclusion: ABot-Earth 0.5 提供了一种超低成本、高效的解决方案，仅利用广泛可获取的卫星影像即可合成大规模、高保真的3D环境。该框架不仅大幅降低了全球尺度3D重建的门槛，还通过弥合仿真与现实的差距赋能了具身AI应用，为全球数字地球可视化的未来发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present ABot-Earth 0.5, a generative 3D framework designed to synthesize vast, seamless 3D environments from ubiquitous, geospatially referenced satellite imagery. To achieve this, we propose a novel generative model formulated directly with the 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) representation. The model is trained on a diverse corpus of existing real-world urban reconstructions, learning to generate realistic geometry and textures. At inference, it synthesizes novel 3D scenes conditioned solely on satellite imagery at a scalable rate of under 10 minutes per square kilometer, while demonstrating exceptional realism. The framework is designed for accessibility, with integrated hierarchical level-of-detail (LOD) structures that permit real-time, interactive visualization on web-based map engines. This high-fidelity simulation sandbox effectively mitigates the sim-to-real domain gap, enabling critical downstream Embodied AI applications like closed-loop UAV navigation. By providing an ultra-low-cost and high-efficiency solution, ABot-Earth 0.5 significantly lowers the technical and financial barriers to large-scale 3D reconstruction and empowers the future of global digital earth visualization.

</details>


### [128] [Generalized-CVO: Fast and Correspondence-Free Local Point Cloud Registration with Second Order Riemannian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10019)
*Ray Zhang,Marcus Greiff,Thomas Lew,John Subosits*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于各向异性核与RKHS嵌入的无需对应关系的局部点云配准方法，结合二阶流形优化实现高效精确配准，在LiDAR和RGB-D跟踪任务中显著提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RKHS的无对应关系点云配准方法使用一阶优化器，收敛速度慢；同时传统方法未充分利用局部几何结构信息（如曲面法线与切线方向的差异），导致在特征稀疏环境中配准精度不佳。

Method: 1) 使用逐点各向异性核将点云表示为连续函数，编码局部几何结构，使配准沿法线方向加强对齐约束、沿切线方向放松约束；2) 提出基于近似黎曼Hessian的二阶流形优化方案求解配准问题，相比一阶方法获得高达10倍的加速。

Result: 在多种室内外数据集的帧间LiDAR和RGB-D跟踪任务中提升了精度；在驾驶场景的LiDAR跟踪任务中，于特征稀疏环境下平移和旋转漂移均降低超过55%；在物体配准基准上，相比ICP方法展现更强的鲁棒性，在中等初始错位条件下精化全局初始化时获得进一步提升。

Conclusion: 结合各向异性几何编码与二阶流形优化的无对应关系RKHS配准方法，在速度和精度上均优于先前方法，尤其在特征稀疏和中等错位场景下表现突出，适用于LiDAR和RGB-D的多种配准应用。

Abstract: We propose a fast and correspondence-free local point cloud registration method that leverages geometric surface structure and reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) embeddings. The method represents point clouds as continuous functions with point-wise anisotropic kernels that encode local geometry. This formulation improves alignment along surface normals while relaxing alignment along tangential directions. To solve the resulting registration problem, we propose a second-order on-manifold optimization scheme with approximate Riemannian Hessians, achieving a speedup of up to 10x over the first-order solvers used in prior correspondence-free RKHS-based methods. We demonstrate improved frame-to-frame LiDAR and RGB-D tracking accuracy across diverse indoor and outdoor datasets. On a LiDAR tracking registration task in the driving domain, we achieve a reduction of $>55\%$ in both translational and rotational drift in challenging feature-sparse environments. On object registration benchmarks, we show improved robustness over ICP-based methods and further gains when refining global initialization, particularly under moderate misalignment.

</details>


### [129] [SpineReport: Automated 3D Quantification and Reporting of Lumbar Spine Degeneration on MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10021)
*Nathan Molinier,Adrian A. Marth,Reto Sutter,Christoph Germann,Jacob A. Connolly,Mathieu Guay-Paquet,Nathan D. Schilaty,Kenneth A. Weber,Julien Cohen-Adad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpineReport是一个开源的全自动框架，用于腰椎MRI的综合三维形态学分析，能提取定量指标并生成受试者特定报告，在中央椎管狭窄评估中表现优异（AUC=0.95）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腰椎疾病是全球致残的主要原因，但从MRI可靠量化退变仍具挑战性。临床实践中主要采用二维分析，因三维手动评估耗时；而二维测量的可重复性有限，尤其当解剖结构与成像平面不对齐时。现有自动化方法多局限于二维、依赖离散分级，或缺乏鲁棒性和可解释性。因此需要一种全自动、三维、可量化且可解释的腰椎MRI分析工具。

Method: 提出SpineReport框架，基于鲁棒的解剖分割，从椎管、脊髓、椎体、椎间盘和椎间孔等关键结构中提取三维定量指标，包括形态学特征和信号特征（如T2加权CSF信号）。支持跨受试者和纵向评估，并生成受试者特定报告，允许与队列分布进行比较，提升可解释性和脊柱形态的客观表征。

Result: 提取的指标与中央椎管狭窄严重程度呈强相关，T2加权CSF信号表现最佳（AUC=0.95），椎管前后径和面积比也表现出强相关性和高判别力（AUC>0.80）。对于侧隐窝狭窄，相关性中等，侧方CSF信号最具信息量（AUC=0.73）。尽管感兴趣区提取鲁棒，但椎间孔狭窄未观察到显著相关性。

Conclusion: SpineReport提供了一个开源、全自动的腰椎MRI三维形态学分析工具，能有效量化脊柱退变，尤其在中央椎管狭窄评估方面表现出色。该工具通过生成可与人群分布对比的个体化报告，提升了临床可解释性，但在椎间孔狭窄评估方面仍需改进。

Abstract: Lumbar spine conditions are a leading cause of disability worldwide, yet reliable quantification of degeneration from MRI remains challenging. In clinical practice, analysis is predominantly performed in two dimensions (2D), as manual three-dimensional (3D) assessment is time-consuming. However, 2D measurements suffer from limited reproducibility, particularly when anatomical structures are not aligned with the imaging plane. Existing automated approaches are often restricted to 2D, rely on discrete grading, or lack robustness and interpretability. We introduce SpineReport, an open-source, fully automated framework for comprehensive 3D morphometric analysis of lumbar spine MRI. Leveraging robust anatomical segmentations, the method extracts quantitative metrics from key structures, including the spinal canal, spinal cord, vertebrae, intervertebral discs, and foramina. These include both morphological and signal-based features, enabling cross-subject and longitudinal assessment. SpineReport further generates subject-specific reports that allow comparison with cohort distributions, improving interpretability and objective characterization of spinal morphology. Clinical relevance was evaluated against radiologist-reported severity grades for central canal, lateral recess, and foraminal stenosis. Metrics showed strong associations with central canal stenosis severity, with T2-weighted CSF signal providing the highest performance (AUC = 0.95). Canal AP diameter and area ratios also demonstrated strong correlations and high discriminative ability (AUC > 0.80). For lateral recess stenosis, associations were moderate, with lateral CSF signal being the most informative (AUC = 0.73). No significant associations were observed for foraminal stenosis despite robust region-of-interest extraction. SpineReport is released as an open-access tool: https://ivadomed.github.io/SpineReport/

</details>


### [130] [A Controlled Audit of Pretraining Contamination in Public Medical Vision-Language Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10066)
*Bruce Changlong Xu,Lan Wu,Alexander Ryu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文审计了医学视觉语言模型(VLM)在公开基准上的数据污染问题，发现SLAKE-En存在可测量的图像源重叠和文本侧规范序可交换性信号，但基于队列的Min-K%++和跨模型top-K重叠检测器在小规模医学VLM队列上不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在公开基准（如SLAKE-En、PathVQA、VQA-RAD等）上评估性能时，默认假设这些基准数据未出现在预训练中，但这些数据集已公开可下载多年，存在数据污染的风险。论文旨在系统性地审计开源医学VLM是否存在基准数据泄露问题，并评估现有污染检测方法的可靠性。

Method: 采用四类检测器家族进行审计：(1) 图像侧近邻重叠检测——将基准图像与PMC-OA-beta进行比对；(2) 规范序可交换性检测——检验模型对选项顺序的敏感性；(3) 基于队列的Min-K%++尾部富集检测——衡量模型对基准文本的异常低困惑度；(4) 跨模型top-K重叠检测——比较不同模型间高置信预测的一致性。同时引入域外控制组和外部非医学基线（如BLIP-2）来验证检测器的有效性。

Result: 图像侧：SLAKE-En中19.8%（SigLIP-B-16）和4.2%（SigLIP-SO400M）的图像被标记为与PMC-OA-beta存在重叠，域外控制组为0/2000；人工审核显示为同模态、同投影但不同患者的匹配，属于源/分布重叠而非像素级重复。文本侧：Qwen2.5-VL在SLAKE-En上显示出经排序消融和外部基线验证后仍存在的规范序可交换性信号；在OmniMedVQA镜像上，五个模型触发可交换性信号而BLIP-2保持干净。然而，Min-K%++尾部富集和跨模型top-K重叠信号在引入BLIP-2外部基线后崩溃——BLIP-2在缺乏医学VQA暴露的情况下复现了这些表面阳性信号。

Conclusion: 基于队列的Min-K%++和跨模型top-K重叠检测器作为独立的成员推断信号在小规模医学VLM队列上不可靠。SLAKE-En存在可测量的图像源重叠和文本侧污染信号，但应被解释为分布重叠而非确认的逐样本记忆。医学VLM基准评估的公正性面临挑战，需要更审慎地使用和解释污染检测工具。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) are evaluated on public benchmarks whose images and question-answer pairs have been freely downloadable for years, yet reported accuracy assumes these examples were absent from pretraining. We audit open VLMs on SLAKE-En, PathVQA, VQA-RAD, and an auxiliary public OmniMedVQA mirror using four detector families: image-side near-neighbour overlap against PMC-OA-beta, canonical-order exchangeability, cohort-relative Min-K%++ tail enrichment, and cross-model top-K overlap. We find measurable image-side source overlap on SLAKE-En: 19.8% of images are flagged under SigLIP-B-16 and 4.2% under SigLIP-SO400M, while out-of-domain controls produce 0/2000 flags. Manual adjudication shows same-modality, same-projection matches to different patients rather than verified pixel-level duplicates, so we interpret this as source or distributional overlap rather than confirmed per-image memorization. On the text side, Qwen2.5-VL on SLAKE-En shows a canonical-order exchangeability signal that survives ordering ablation and external non-medical baselines. On the OmniMedVQA mirror, exchangeability fires for five medical and general VLMs while BLIP-2 remains clean. In contrast, cohort-relative Min-K%++ tail enrichment and cross-model top-K overlap collapse under an external pre-domain baseline: BLIP-2 reproduces the apparent positive signals despite lacking plausible medical-VQA exposure. We conclude that these cohort-relative detectors are unreliable as standalone membership-inference signals on small medical-VLM cohorts.

</details>


### [131] [Interpretable Temporal Facial-Region Motion Analysis for In-the-Wild Parkinson's Disease Video Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10088)
*Riyadh Almushrafy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用面部关键点的时间运动描述符，在YouTubePD基准上实现了帕金森病相关视频的自动分类，归一化速度描述符配合随机森林分类器达到了0.826的平衡准确率和0.855的AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病（PD）常伴有面部表情减少（面具脸/面部运动迟缓），本文旨在探索从面部区域关键点提取的时间运动描述符能否在自然环境（in-the-wild）条件下，支持YouTubePD基准上的PD相关视频二分类任务，寻求一种轻量且可解释的表征方法。

Method: 从14个预定义面部区域提取几何描述符表征每个视频，系统比较了五种表征方式：静态几何、归一化几何、基于速度的描述符、相对速度描述符以及GRU序列基线模型，均在相同的二分类协议下评估。此外，还进行了种子鲁棒性分析、区域级消融实验和排列重要性分析，以评估模型的稳定性和可解释性。

Result: 归一化速度描述符结合随机森林分类器取得最佳结果，在测试集上平衡准确率为0.826，AUROC为0.855。在10个随机种子下表现稳定，平衡准确率为0.810±0.018，AUROC为0.855±0.005。

Conclusion: 归一化面部区域运动是一种轻量且可解释的表征方法，适用于YouTubePD视频分类任务。本研究定位为基准级分析，不声称具备临床严重程度评估或MDS-UPDRS面部表情评分的能力。

Abstract: Reduced facial expressivity is a common motor manifestation of Parkinson's disease (PD), often described as hypomimia or facial bradykinesia. This paper examines whether temporal motion descriptors extracted from facial-region keypoints can support in-the-wild PD-related video classification on the YouTubePD benchmark. Each video is represented using geometric descriptors from 14 predefined facial regions. Static geometry, normalized geometry, velocity-based descriptors, relative-velocity descriptors, and a GRU sequence baseline are compared under the same binary classification protocol. To assess stability and interpretability, the study includes seed-robustness analysis, region-level ablation, and permutation importance. The best result is obtained with normalized velocity descriptors and a Random Forest classifier, reaching a balanced accuracy of 0.826 and an AUROC of 0.855 on the held-out test split. Across 10 random seeds, this representation remains stable, with balanced accuracy of 0.810 +/- 0.018 and AUROC of 0.855 +/- 0.005. Overall, the results suggest that normalized facial-region motion is a lightweight and interpretable representation for YouTubePD video classification. The study is framed as a benchmark-level analysis and does not claim clinical severity assessment or MDS-UPDRS facial-expression scoring.

</details>


### [132] [Maximum Matching Accuracy: An Instance Segmentation Evaluation Metric Utilizing Globally Optimal Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10107)
*Kaden Stillwagon,Alexandra D. VandeLoo,Craig R. Forest*

Main category: cs.CV
